const createDefaultOptions = (): CreateStoreOptions => ({
  paths: [],
  reducers: {},
  preloadedState: undefined,
  middleware: [],
  compose: reduxDevToolsCompose,
  enhancers: [],
  plugins: [],

export function createStore(opts = {}): Store | void {
  const context = getContext()

  if (!context) {
    console.error('[KEA] Can not create a store without a Kea context.')
    return
  }

  if (context['__store']) {
    console.error('[KEA] Already attached to a store! Exiting. Please reset the kea context before creating a store.')
    return
  }

  // clone options
  const options = { ...createDefaultOptions(), ...opts }

  // clone redux reducers
  context.reducers.redux = { ...options.reducers }

  // run pre-hooks
  runPlugins('beforeReduxStore', options)

  // combine middleware into the first enhancer
  if (options.middleware.length > 0) {
    options.enhancers = ([applyMiddleware(...options.middleware)] as StoreEnhancer[]).concat(options.enhancers)
  }

  // use a special compose function?
  const composeEnhancer: typeof compose = options.compose || compose

  // create the store creator
  const finalCreateStore = composeEnhancer(
    pauseListenersEnhancer,
    ...options.enhancers,

STEP 46 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'EOF'
import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { render, screen, act } from '@testing-library/react'
import { kea, resetContext, getContext, actions, reducers, selectors, path, useValues } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('selectorHealth is undefined when atomicSelectors disabled', () => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true })
    const logic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        double: [(s) => [s.count], (count) => count * 2],
      }),
      reducers: () => ({ count: [1, {}] }),
    })
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth).toBe(undefined)
    expect(logic().selectorHealth).toBe(undefined)
  })

  test('selectorHealth is a function when enabled', () => {
    const logic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        double: [(s) => [s.count], (count) => count * 2],
      }),
      reducers: () => ({ count: [1, {}] }),
    })
    logic.mount()
    expect(typeof logic.selectorHealth).toBe('function')
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.count).toBeDefined()
    expect(health.selectors.double).toBeDefined()
    expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['count', 'double'])
  })

  test('tracks dependencies at leaf level (user.name vs user.age)', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    let nameEvals = 0
    const evalLogic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => {
            nameEvals += 1
            return user.name
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = evalLogic.mount()
    expect(evalLogic.values.userName).toBe('a')
    const before = evalLogic.selectorHealth()
    expect(before.selectors.userName.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(before.selectors.userName.dependencies).not.toContain('user')
    expect(before.selectors.userName.dependencies).not.toContain('user.age')

    const evalBefore = evalLogic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations
    evalLogic.actions.setAge(2)
    // user.age changed, user.name did not -> no re-evaluation
    expect(evalLogic.values.userName).toBe('a')
    expect(evalLogic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(evalBefore)

    evalLogic.actions.setName('b')
    expect(evalLogic.values.userName).toBe('b')
    expect(evalLogic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(evalBefore + 1)

    unmount()
    // second logic not mounted, just ensure it builds
    expect(logic.pathString).toBeTruthy()
  })

  test('supports Map key access dependencies', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setMap: (map) => ({ map }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 2]]), {
          setMap: (state, { map }) => map,
        }],
      }),
      selectors({
        valueA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.get('a')],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.valueA).toBe(1)
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.valueA.dependencies).toContain('data.map:a')
    expect(health.selectors.valueA.dependencies).not.toContain('data.map:b')
  })

  test('supports Set membership dependencies', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setSet: (set) => ({ set }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Set(['a', 'b']), {
          setSet: (state, { set }) => set,
        }],
      }),
      selectors({
        hasA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.has('a')],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.hasA.dependencies).toContain('data.set:a')
    expect(health.selectors.hasA.dependencies).not.toContain('data.set:b')
  })

  test('supports Array index and includes dependencies', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setList: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({
        list: [[10, 20, 30], {
          setList: (state, { list }) => list,
        }],
      }),
      selectors({
        first: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list[0]],
        hasTen: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list.includes(10)],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.first.dependencies).toContain('list.0')
    expect(health.selectors.hasTen.dependencies).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['list.0', 'list.1', 'list.2']))
  })

  test('propagates through multi-level chains only to affected selectors', () => {
    let lowerEvals = 0
    let middleEvals = 0
    let upperEvals = 0

    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setLower: (v) => ({ v }) }),
      reducers({
        lower: ['a', { setLower: (_, { v }) => v }],
      }),
      selectors({
        middle: [(s) => [s.lower], (lower) => {
          middleEvals += 1
          return lower.toUpperCase()
        }],
        upper: [(s) => [s.middle], (middle) => {
          upperEvals += 1
          return middle + '!'
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.upper).toBe('A!')
    expect(middleEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(upperEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setLower('b')
    expect(logic.values.upper).toBe('B!')
    expect(middleEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(upperEvals).toBe(2)

    // No changes -> no re-evaluations
    logic.actions.setLower('b')
    expect(logic.values.upper).toBe('B!')
    expect(middleEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(upperEvals).toBe(2)

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.middle.dependencies).toContain('lower')
    expect(health.selectors.upper.dependencies).toContain('middle')
    expect(health.selectors.lower.dependents).toContain('middle')
    expect(health.selectors.middle.dependents).toContain('upper')
    expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['lower', 'middle', 'upper'])
  })

  test('atomic updates trigger exactly one re-evaluation', () => {
    let evals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setBoth: (name, age) => ({ name, age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setBoth: (state, { name, age }) => ({ ...state, name, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userInfo: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => {
          evals += 1
          return `${user.name}:${user.age}`
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.userInfo).toBe('a:1')
    expect(evals).toBe(1)
    logic.actions.setBoth('b', 2)
    expect(logic.values.userInfo).toBe('b:2')
    expect(evals).toBe(2)
    // Calling values again should not re-evaluate
    expect(logic.values.userInfo).toBe('b:2')
    expect(evals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('detects circular dependencies during build', () => {
    const circularLogic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b],
        b: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a],
      }),
    })
    expect(() => circularLogic()).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  })

  test('exposes dirtyCause', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
        greeting: [(s) => [s.userName], (userName) => `hi ${userName}`],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.greeting).toBe('hi a')
    logic.actions.setName('b')
    expect(logic.values.greeting).toBe('hi b')
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.userName.dirtyCause).toBe('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.greeting.dirtyCause).toBe('selector:userName')
  })

  test('react components only re-render for accessed changes', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    let renders = 0
    function NameComponent() {
      renders += 1
      const { userName } = useValues(logic)
      return <div data-testid="name">{userName}</div>
    }

    render(<NameComponent />)
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name')).toHaveTextContent('a')
    const rendersAfterMount = renders

    act(() => logic.actions.setAge(2))
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name')).toHaveTextContent('a')
    expect(renders).toBe(rendersAfterMount)

    act(() => logic.actions.setName('b'))
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name')).toHaveTextContent('b')
    expect(renders).toBe(rendersAfterMount + 1)
  })

  test('does not disrupt plugin lifecycle ordering', () => {
    const order = []
    const testPlugin = {
      name: 'atomic-test-plugin',
      events: {
        afterLogic(logic) { order.push(`afterLogic:${logic.pathString}`) },
        beforeMount(logic) { order.push(`beforeMount:${logic.pathString}`) },
        afterMount(logic) { order.push(`afterMount:${logic.pathString}`) },
      },
    }
    getContext().plugins.activated.push(testPlugin)
    getContext().plugins.events.afterLogic.push(testPlugin.events.afterLogic)
    getContext().plugins.events.beforeMount.push(testPlugin.events.beforeMount)
    getContext().plugins.events.afterMount.push(testPlugin.events.afterMount)

    const logic = kea([
      path(['atomic', 'lifecycle']),
      reducers({ value: [1, {}] }),
      selectors({ doubled: [(s) => [s.value], (value) => value * 2] }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(order).toEqual([
      'afterLogic:atomic.lifecycle',
      'beforeMount:atomic.lifecycle',
      'afterMount:atomic.lifecycle',
    ])
  })
})
EOF
echo "test written"
OUTPUT:
test written

import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, getContext } from '../src'

test('debug', () => {
  resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  const logic = kea([
    actions({ setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
    reducers({
      user: [{ name: 'a', age: 1 }, { setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }) }],
    }),
    selectors({
      userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
    }),
  ])
  const built = logic()
  built.mount()
  console.log('state', getContext().store.getState())
  console.log('path', built.path, built.pathString)
  console.log('root', built.selector(getContext().store.getState()))
  try {
    console.log('userName', built.values.userName)
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('ERR', e.message, e.stack)
  }
})

COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='test/jest/atomic-selectors.js'
s=open(p).read()
# Insert additional tests before the final lifecycle test
marker = "  test('does not disrupt plugin lifecycle ordering', () => {"
addition = '''  test('Map changes only invalidate selectors reading that key', () => {
    let valueAEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setMapValue: (key, value) => ({ key, value }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [
          new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 2]]),
          {
            setMapValue: (state, { key, value }) => {
              const next = new Map(state)
              next.set(key, value)
              return next
            },
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        valueA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => {
          valueAEvals += 1
          return data.get('a')
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.valueA).toBe(1)
    expect(valueAEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setMapValue('b', 22)
    expect(logic.values.valueA).toBe(1)
    expect(valueAEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setMapValue('a', 11)
    expect(logic.values.valueA).toBe(11)
    expect(valueAEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('Set membership changes only invalidate selectors reading that value', () => {
    let hasAEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ toggle: (value) => ({ value }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [
          new Set(['a']),
          {
            toggle: (state, { value }) => {
              const next = new Set(state)
              if (next.has(value)) next.delete(value)
              else next.add(value)
              return next
            },
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        hasA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => {
          hasAEvals += 1
          return data.has('a')
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
    expect(hasAEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.toggle('b')
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
    expect(hasAEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.toggle('a')
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(false)
    expect(hasAEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('Array changes only invalidate selectors reading changed indices', () => {
    let firstEvals = 0
    let hasEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setIndex: (index, value) => ({ index, value }) }),
      reducers({
        list: [
          [10, 20, 30],
          {
            setIndex: (state, { index, value }) => state.map((v, i) => (i === index ? value : v)),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        first: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => {
          firstEvals += 1
          return list[0]
        }],
        hasTen: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => {
          hasEvals += 1
          return list.includes(10)
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setIndex(1, 99)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setIndex(0, 42)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(42)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(false)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('detects indirect circular dependencies', () => {
    const circularLogic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b],
        b: [(s) => [s.c], (c) => c],
        c: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a],
      }),
    })
    expect(() => circularLogic()).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  })

'''
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, addition + marker)
open(p,'w').write(s)

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > test/jest/debug2.test.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

test('debug array', () => {
  resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  let firstEvals = 0
  let hasEvals = 0
  const logic = kea([
    actions({ setIndex: (index, value) => ({ index, value }) }),
    reducers({
      list: [[10, 20, 30], { setIndex: (state, { index, value }) => state.map((v, i) => (i === index ? value : v)) }],
    }),
    selectors({
      first: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => { firstEvals += 1; return list[0] }],
      hasTen: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => { hasEvals += 1; return list.includes(10) }],
    }),
  ])
  logic.mount()
  console.log('first', logic.values.first, 'fe', firstEvals)
  console.log('health after first', JSON.stringify(logic.selectorHealth(), null, 2))
  console.log('hasTen', logic.values.hasTen, 'he', hasEvals)
  console.log('health after hasTen', JSON.stringify(logic.selectorHealth(), null, 2))
})
EOF

    expect(hasAEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('Array changes only invalidate selectors reading changed indices', () => {
    let firstEvals = 0
    let hasEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setIndex: (index, value) => ({ index, value }) }),
      reducers({
        list: [
          [10, 20, 30],
          {
            setIndex: (state, { index, value }) => state.map((v, i) => (i === index ? value : v)),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        first: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => {
          firstEvals += 1
          return list[0]
        }],
        hasTen: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => {
          hasEvals += 1
          return list.includes(10)
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setIndex(1, 99)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setIndex(0, 42)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(42)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(false)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('detects indirect circular dependencies', () => {
    const circularLogic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b],
        b: [(s) => [s.c], (c) => c],
        c: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a],
      }),
    })
    expect(() => circularLogic()).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  })

    const order = []
    const testPlugin = {
      name: 'atomic-test-plugin',

p='test/jest/atomic-selectors.js'
s=open(p).read()
old = """    logic.actions.setIndex(1, 99)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(1)

    expect(firstEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(2)
"""
new = """    logic.actions.setIndex(1, 99)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(true)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(1)
    // `.includes()` checks every element, so a change at any index re-evaluates it.
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(2)

    logic.actions.setIndex(0, 42)
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe(42)
    expect(logic.values.hasTen).toBe(false)
    expect(firstEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(hasEvals).toBe(3)
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

    let userInfoEvals = 0
    let nameEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'a', age: 1 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userInfo: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => {
          userInfoEvals += 1
          return { name: user.name, age: user.age }
        }],
        nameOnly: [(s) => [s.userInfo], (info) => {
          nameEvals += 1
          return info.name
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.nameOnly).toBe('a')
    expect(userInfoEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(1)

    // user.age changes: userInfo re-evaluates, but nameOnly should not
    logic.actions.setAge(2)
    expect(logic.values.nameOnly).toBe('a')
    expect(userInfoEvals).toBe(2)
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(1)

    // user.name changes: both re-evaluate
    logic.actions.setName('b')
    expect(logic.values.nameOnly).toBe('b')
    expect(userInfoEvals).toBe(3)
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(2)

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.userInfo.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.userInfo.dependencies).toContain('user.age')
    expect(health.selectors.nameOnly.dependencies).toContain('userInfo.name')
  })

  test('tracks nested Map value access', () => {
    let nameEvals = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setMapValue: (key, value) => ({ key, value }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [
          new Map([['a', { name: 'x', age: 1 }]]),
          {
            setMapValue: (state, { key, value }) => {
              const next = new Map(state)
              next.set(key, value)
              return next
            },
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        nameA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => {
          nameEvals += 1
          return data.get('a').name
        }],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.nameA).toBe('x')
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(1)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.nameA.dependencies).toContain('data.map:a.name')

    logic.actions.setMapValue('a', { name: 'x', age: 2 })
    expect(logic.values.nameA).toBe('x')
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setMapValue('a', { name: 'y', age: 2 })
    expect(logic.values.nameA).toBe('y')
    expect(nameEvals).toBe(2)
  })

  test('re-evaluates selectors when props change', () => {
    const logic = kea({
      selectors: () => ({
        id: [(s, p) => [p.id], (id) => id],
      }),
    })
    const built = logic({ id: 1 })
    built.mount()
    expect(built.values.id).toBe(1)

    logic({ id: 2 })
    expect(built.values.id).toBe(2)
  })

  test('works with connected values', () => {
    const source = kea([
      actions({ setCount: (count) => ({ count }) }),
      reducers({ count: [1, { setCount: (_, { count }) => count }] }),
    ])
    const target = kea([
      connect,
    ])
    // Use a manual connect since the builder is imported below
    const { connect } = require('../../src')
    const targetLogic = kea([
      connect({ values: [source, ['count']] }),
      selectors({
        doubled: [(s) => [s.count], (count) => count * 2],
      }),
    ])
    targetLogic.mount()
    expect(targetLogic.values.doubled).toBe(2)
    source.actions.setCount(3)
    expect(targetLogic.values.doubled).toBe(6)
  })

'''

STEP 67 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Add maximalPaths helper after firstSegment
old = """function firstSegment(path: string): string {
  const index = path.indexOf('.')
  return index === -1 ? path : path.slice(0, index)
}
"""
new = """function firstSegment(path: string): string {
  const index = path.indexOf('.')
  return index === -1 ? path : path.slice(0, index)
}

/** Remove paths that are ancestors of other recorded paths (e.g. keep `user.name`, drop `user`). */
function maximalPaths(paths: Iterable<string>): string[] {
  const list = [...paths]
  return list.filter((path) => {
    const prefix = `${path}.`
    return !list.some((other) => other !== path && other.startsWith(prefix))
  })
}
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

# Update commitDeps to refine stateDeps and pendingDeps
old = """    const dependencies = new Set<string>()
    const depKinds = new Map<string, DepKind>()
    for (const path of session.stateDeps) {
      dependencies.add(path)
      depKinds.set(path, 'state')
    }
    for (const entry of session.pendingDeps.values()) {
      if (entry.paths.size === 0) {
        dependencies.add(entry.selector)
        depKinds.set(entry.selector, 'selector')
      } else {
        for (const path of entry.paths) {
          dependencies.add(path)
          depKinds.set(path, 'state')
        }
      }
    }
"""
new = """    const dependencies = new Set<string>()
    const depKinds = new Map<string, DepKind>()
    for (const path of maximalPaths(session.stateDeps)) {
      dependencies.add(path)
      depKinds.set(path, 'state')
    }
    for (const entry of session.pendingDeps.values()) {
      if (entry.paths.size === 0) {
        dependencies.add(entry.selector)
        depKinds.set(entry.selector, 'selector')
      } else {
        for (const path of maximalPaths(entry.paths)) {
          dependencies.add(path)
          depKinds.set(path, 'state')
        }
      }
    }
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print('refinement added')
PY
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | head -20
OUTPUT:
refinement added

STEP 70 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

# Add markDirtyForChanges method after propagate
old = """  private propagate(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, oldVal: any, newVal: any): void {
    if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, meta.name)
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }
"""
new = """  private propagate(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, oldVal: any, newVal: any): void {
    if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, meta.name)
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
    this.markDirtyForChanges(changes, meta.name)
  }

  private markDirtyForChanges(changes: string[], sourceSelector?: string): void {
    for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
      if (sourceSelector && meta.name === sourceSelector) {
        continue
      }
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        const matching = changes.find((change) => dep === change || change.startsWith(`${dep}.`))
        if (matching) {
          const kind = meta.depKinds.get(dep)
          meta.dirtyCause = kind === 'selector' ? `selector:${dep}` : matching
          break
        }
      }
    }
  }
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

# In notify, after bumping changes, mark dirty causes
old = """    const changes = diffPaths(previousRoot, nextRoot, '')
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }
"""
new = """    const changes = diffPaths(previousRoot, nextRoot, '')
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
    this.markDirtyForChanges(changes)
  }
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print('eager dirtyCause added')

  input: SelectorDefinitions<L> | ((logic: L) => SelectorDefinitions<L>),
): LogicBuilder<L> {
  return (logic) => {
    const selectorInputs = typeof input === 'function' ? input(logic) : input

    if (getContext().options.atomicSelectors) {
      atomicSelectorsBuilder(logic, selectorInputs)
      return
    }

    // small cache so the order would not count

        throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" already exists`)
      }

--- atomic builder ---
  const engine = getAtomicEngine(logic as any)

  const propSelectors =
    typeof Proxy !== 'undefined'
      ? new Proxy(logic.props, {
          get(target, prop) {
            if (!(prop in target)) {
              throw new Error(
                `[KEA] Prop "${String(prop)}" not found for logic "${
                  logic.pathString
                }". Attempted to use in a selector. Please specify a default via props({ ${String(
                  prop,
                )}: '' }) to resolve.`,
              )
            }
            return () => target[prop]
          },
        })
      : (Object.fromEntries(
          Object.keys(logic.props).map((key) => [key, () => logic.props[key]]),
        ) as LogicPropSelectors<L>)

  const entries: { name: string; args: Selector[]; func: (...args: any[]) => any }[] = []
  const staticDependencies = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
  let currentName: string | null = null

  const selectorsProxy = new Proxy(logic.selectors, {
    get(_target, prop, receiver) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(_target, prop, receiver)
      }
      const name = String(prop)
      if (currentName) {
        if (!staticDependencies.has(currentName)) {
          staticDependencies.set(currentName, new Set())
        }
        staticDependencies.get(currentName)!.add(name)
      }
      return engine.callSelectorFunction(name)
    },
  })

  for (const entry of Object.entries(selectorInputs)) {
    const [key, arr]: [string, SelectorDefinition<L['selectors'], LogicPropSelectors<L>, any> | undefined] = entry
    if (typeof logic.selectors[key] !== 'undefined') {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" already exists`)
    }
    if (!arr) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" is undefined`)
    }
    const [input, func] = arr
    currentName = key
    const args: Selector[] = input(selectorsProxy as any, propSelectors)
    currentName = null

    if (args.filter((a) => typeof a !== 'function').length > 0) {
      const argTypes = args.map((a) => typeof a).join(', ')
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}", selector "${key}" has incorrect input: [${argTypes}].`)
    }
    entries.push({ name: key, args, func })
  }

  detectCircularSelectorDependencies(staticDependencies)

  for (const { name, args, func } of entries) {
    addSelectorAndValue(logic, name, engine.createSelector(name, args, func))
  }
}

export function addSelectorAndValue<L extends Logic = Logic>(logic: L, key: string, selector: Selector): void {
  logic.selectors[key] = selector
  if (!logic.values.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
    Object.defineProperty(logic.values, key, {
      get: function () {
        return logic.selectors[key](getStoreState(), logic.props)
      },
      enumerable: true,
    })
  }
}

import { BuiltLogic, Selector } from '../types'
import { getContext, getStoreState } from '../kea/context'

type DepKind = 'selector' | 'state'

interface AtomicSelectorMeta {
  name: string
  args: Selector[]
  func: (...args: any[]) => any
  dependencies: Set<string>
  depKinds: Map<string, DepKind>
  lastDepVersions: Map<string, number>
  lastArgResults: any[]
  lastPropsVersion: number
  cached: any
  hasCached: boolean
  evaluations: number
  dirtyCause: string | null
  inProgress: boolean
  isReducer?: boolean
}

interface PendingDep {
  selector: string
  whole: boolean
  paths: Set<string>
}

type Recorder = (path: string) => void

const proxyTargets = new WeakMap<object, any>()

function joinPath(base: string, key: string): string {
  return base ? `${base}.${key}` : key
}

function isTrackableObject(value: any): boolean {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object'
}

function registerProxy(proxy: object, target: any): any {
  proxyTargets.set(proxy, target)
  return proxy
}

function unwrap(value: any): any {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && proxyTargets.has(value)) {
    return proxyTargets.get(value)
  }
  return value
}

function createObjectProxy(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      const key = String(prop)
      // Advanced array methods: `.includes()` checks every element.
      if (Array.isArray(target) && key === 'includes') {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(i)))
        }
        return target.includes.bind(target)
      }
      // Reading the length depends on the whole collection.
      if (key === 'length') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.length
      }
      const result = Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      const childPath = joinPath(basePath, key)
      recorder(childPath)
      if (isTrackableObject(result)) {
        return createProxy(result, childPath, recorder)
      }
      return result
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol) {
...
      this.pendingDeps.set(name, entry)
    }
    return entry
  }

  wrapSelectorResult(name: string, value: any): any {
    const entry = this.recordSelectorDep(name)
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
      return createProxy(value, name, (path: string) => {
        entry.whole = false
        entry.paths.add(path)
      })
    }
    return value
  }
}

function diffPaths(oldVal: any, newVal: any, base: string): string[] {
  if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
    return []
  }
  if (!isTrackableObject(oldVal) || !isTrackableObject(newVal)) {
    return [base]
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Map && newVal instanceof Map) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const keys = new Set([...oldVal.keys(), ...newVal.keys()])
    for (const key of keys) {
      const keyPath = joinPath(base, `map:${String(key)}`)
      const oldHas = oldVal.has(key)
      const newHas = newVal.has(key)
      if (!oldHas || !newHas || !Object.is(oldVal.get(key), newVal.get(key))) {
        result.push(keyPath)
      }
      if (oldHas && newHas) {
        const oldValue = oldVal.get(key)
        const newValue = newVal.get(key)
        if (!Object.is(oldValue, newValue) && isTrackableObject(oldValue) && isTrackableObject(newValue)) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldValue, newValue, keyPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Set && newVal instanceof Set) {
    const result: string[] = []
    for (const value of oldVal) {
      if (!newVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    for (const value of newVal) {
      if (!oldVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (Array.isArray(oldVal) && Array.isArray(newVal)) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const length = Math.max(oldVal.length, newVal.length)
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      if (!Object.is(oldVal[i], newVal[i])) {
        const indexPath = joinPath(base, String(i))
        result.push(indexPath)
        if (isTrackableObject(oldVal[i]) && isTrackableObject(newVal[i])) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldVal[i], newVal[i], indexPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  // Plain objects (and class instances such as Date)
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(oldVal), ...Object.keys(newVal)])
  if (keys.size === 0) {
    return [base]
  }
  const result: string[] = []
  for (const key of keys) {
    const oldChild = oldVal[key]
    const newChild = newVal[key]
    if (!Object.is(oldChild, newChild)) {
      const childPath = joinPath(base, key)
      if (isTrackableObject(oldChild) && isTrackableObject(newChild)) {
        result.push(...diffPaths(oldChild, newChild, childPath))
      } else {
        result.push(childPath)
      }
    }
  }
  return result
}

function splitPath(path: string): string[] {
  return path.split('.')
}

function firstSegment(path: string): string {
  const index = path.indexOf('.')
  return index === -1 ? path : path.slice(0, index)
}

cd /app && sed -n '280,470p' src/core/atomic-selectors.ts
OUTPUT:
}

/** Remove paths that are ancestors of other recorded paths (e.g. keep `user.name`, drop `user`). */
function maximalPaths(paths: Iterable<string>): string[] {
  const list = [...paths]
  return list.filter((path) => {
    const prefix = `${path}.`
    return !list.some((other) => other !== path && other.startsWith(prefix))
  })
}

export class AtomicSelectorEngine {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  versions = new Map<string, number>()
  selectors = new Map<string, AtomicSelectorMeta>()
  currentSession: Session | null = null
  propsVersion = 0

  constructor(logic: BuiltLogic) {
    this.logic = logic
  }

  createReducerSelector(name: string): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args: [],
      func: (root: any) => root[name],
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
      isReducer: true,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any = getStoreState(), props: any = this.logic.props) => this.runReducerSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  createSelector(name: string, args: Selector[], func: (...args: any[]) => any): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args,
      func,
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any = getStoreState(), props: any = this.logic.props) => this.runSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  callSelectorFunction(name: string): Selector {
    const engine = this
    const fn = ((state: any, props: any) => engine.callSelector(name, state, props)) as Selector & {
      _atomicSelectorName: string
    }
    fn._atomicSelectorName = name
    return fn
  }

  callSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const session = this.currentSession
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      const value = this.logic.selectors[name](state, props)
      return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
    }
    const value = meta.isReducer ? this.runReducerSelector(name, state, props) : this.runSelector(name, state, props)
    return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
  }

  private runSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const argResults = meta.args.map((arg) => {
        const argName = (arg as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName) {
          return this.callSelector(argName, state, props)
        }
        return arg(state, props)
      })

      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      let finalDirty = dirty
      let finalCause = cause

      // Selectors that are not part of this engine (e.g. connected values) can not
      // be tracked leaf-by-leaf, so compare their whole result instead.
      for (let i = 0; i < meta.args.length; i++) {
        const argName = (meta.args[i] as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName && !this.selectors.has(argName)) {
          if (!Object.is(meta.lastArgResults[i], argResults[i])) {
            finalDirty = true
            if (!finalCause) {
              finalCause = `selector:${argName}`
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (!finalDirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }

      const result = meta.func(...argResults)
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = meta.dirtyCause !== null ? meta.dirtyCause : finalCause
      meta.lastArgResults = argResults.map(unwrap)
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private runReducerSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic reducer selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      if (!dirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }
      const rootProxy = session.getRoot(state)
      const result = rootProxy[name]
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = meta.dirtyCause !== null ? meta.dirtyCause : cause
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private checkDirty(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta): { dirty: boolean; cause: string | null } {
    if (!meta.hasCached || meta.lastPropsVersion !== this.propsVersion) {
      return { dirty: true, cause: null }
    }
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0
      const last = meta.lastDepVersions.get(dep)
      if (last === undefined || current !== last) {

        return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      }

      return Reflect.has(target, prop)

    ownKeys(target: any) {
      recorder(basePath)
      return Reflect.ownKeys(target)
    },
    getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol) {
      if (typeof prop !== 'symbol') {
        recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(prop)))
      }
      return Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
    },
  })
  return registerProxy(proxy, value)
}

Introduce an opt-in fine-grained reactivity engine for Kea selectors,
enabled via resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true }) and disabled by default.

- Track selector dependencies at exact leaf level (e.g. user.name) so
  unrelated leaf changes do not re-evaluate selectors.
- Support collections: Map key access (data.map:a), Set membership
  (data.set:a), and Array index reads (list.0) plus .includes().
- Propagate updates through multi-level selector chains and evaluate a
  dependent selector at most once per action.
- Detect circular selector dependencies during build/mount and throw
  [KEA] Circular dependency detected.
- Expose logic.selectorHealth() with dependencies, dependents,
  evaluations, dirtyCause and topologicalOrder when enabled; keep it
  undefined when disabled.
- Preserve baseline lifecycle/plugin ordering and React re-render
  behaviour (unrelated state updates do not re-render)." && git log --oneline -3
